Date: 2026-03-11

- Sanitized generated runtime filenames only: accents are transliterated to ASCII, spaces become underscores, and unsupported characters are removed.

Version: 1167
Date: 2026-03-11
Changes:

- Prevented raw Settings radio_name characters from leaking into ssproc/Liquidsoap runtime identifiers.
- Kept the visible Radio name unchanged in Settings; only generated runtime config values are sanitized.

Version: 1168
Date: 2026-03-12
Changes:
- Reworked the Liquidsoap history watcher into one central multi-station watcher that iterates over all running stations by station_key.
- Added station-scoped watcher state, queue-head lookup, and now-playing wake signals so history updates no longer depend on the active station.
- Kept polling lightweight by querying per-station progress only for running stations and only syncing history metadata when needed.

Version: 1169
Date: 2026-03-12
Changes:
- Reworked scheduler execution to process each station database explicitly by station_key instead of the active station only.
- Made history updates rely on Liquidsoap /api/liquidsoap/now_playing track-change callbacks as the primary path for queue-to-history moves.
- Stopped auto-starting the fallback queue watcher to avoid callback/poller cross-station races during playback and scheduling.

Version: 1170
Date: 2026-03-12
Changes:
- Reworked multi-station history handling around a station-local runtime_playback_state so callback-driven track starts are persisted and deduplicated per station.
- Kept /api/liquidsoap/now_playing as the primary history trigger, and added a low-frequency reconciliation safety net that only repairs missed callbacks for the currently playing head track.
- Preserved station_key-based scheduler execution and avoided re-enabling the old aggressive queue watcher path.

Version: 1171
Date: 2026-03-12
Changes:
- Reworked runtime history commits to use exact per-track queue_id/track_id metadata from the generated Liquidsoap playlist, so track-start callbacks no longer rely on fuzzy path/title matching.
- Extended station-local runtime_playback_state with current queue/track commit state, allowing missed callback recovery without swallowing valid new starts or double-writing history.
- Kept the callback as the immediate history trigger at track start, with the reconciler only repairing rare missed inserts for the exact current queue item.

Version: 1172
Date: 2026-03-12
Changes:
- Fixed runtime history commit SQL binding mismatch in playback reconciliation.
- Added safe runtime_playback_state schema migration for callback and commit tracking columns.
- Kept history commit metadata aligned with queue_id and track_id values.

Version: 1174
Date: 2026-03-12
Changes:
- Added structured DEBUG=1 history tracing across queue inserts, Liquidsoap now_playing callbacks, dequeue decisions, history commits, and fallback reconciliation.
- Logged station_key, active station, queue_id, track_id, chosen dequeue reason, and skip/error reasons so missed history entries can be traced end-to-end.
- Kept the added diagnostics DEBUG-only so normal playback behavior and non-debug logs stay unchanged.

Version: 1175
Date: 2026-03-12
Changes:
- Removed active-station fallback from the Liquidsoap now_playing -> dequeue -> history path, so runtime commits only use the explicit callback station_key.
- Refused history/dequeue processing when station_key is missing and added DEBUG trace points for missing station keys.
- Fixed cleanup_dead_process_pids to read the global process PID registry without referencing an undefined station key.

2026-03-12 | v1176
- History: added station-scoped pending history commit state so track-start callbacks are not lost when the queue is temporarily unreadable/empty.
- History: runtime reconciler now retries pending callback-based commits once the queue head becomes readable and matches the pending track.
- History: successful history commit now clears matching pending runtime state.

2026-03-12 | v1177
- History: runtime reconciler now prioritizes pending track-start callback state instead of relying only on now-playing/head path equality.
- History: added pending-versus-head matching by queue_id, track_id, real path, basename, and derived metadata so delayed queue visibility can still commit the correct track.
- History: when a pending callback matches the current queue head, the reconciler now dequeues directly from that pending record without the older store-path mismatch gate.

v1771 - Fixed the remaining Studio Scripts / Scheduler refresh interval in broadcaster.js from 5 seconds to 1 second.

v1772 - Fixed script next-track behavior by forcing an extra station queue sync/reload and a short settle delay before next(). This avoids the race where the inserted top item existed in DB but Liquidsoap still skipped to the old next item.

v1773 - Changed scripted next() to use the same queue-head + skip + dequeue + resync flow as the player Next action, station-scoped. This ensures the inserted top item is synced to the actual M3U before skip, then dequeued and re-synced after the skip.

v1774 - Script next() now uses the same queue-head -> skip -> dequeue -> post-sync flow as the player NEXT action instead of a plain telnet skip. This fixes the case where the time announcement was inserted in time but playback still jumped to the previously prefetched next track.

v1775 - Removed the scripted manual dequeue step after next(). The normal now_playing callback now remains the only owner of queue dequeue/history updates, preventing double-advance over the inserted time-announcement track.

v1776 - Fixed Queue/Playlist Add Files browser regression. The queue/playlist file browser now uses /api/library/files again, while the script browser stays on its separate /api/studio/scripts/browser endpoint for *.wbs files.

v1778 - Corrected both browser loaders explicitly in broadcaster.js: Queue/Playlist Add Files now fetches /api/library/files, while Add Script fetches /api/studio/scripts/browser.

v1779 - Changed scripted time-announcement insertion to match the working manual queue flow: insert at queue end, move the inserted item to first position, sync/reload the station M3U, then perform NEXT.

v1780 - Temporary script-engine test change: next() is disabled for scripted time announcements. The script now only inserts the track, moves it to first position, and syncs the queue/M3U without triggering Next.

- Fixed repeated seek after a seek restart: second and later seeks now use a Python-calculated relative source delta based on the stored seek base and raw Liquidsoap elapsed.
- Prevented Liquidsoap seek_abs from treating a seek-restarted segment as seek_base=0 when metadata is temporarily empty, which could jump 5-10 seconds too far and hit EOF/track change.

Version 2310 - 2026-04-26
- Fixed repeated seek drift after the first seek: direct re-seeks now calculate the relative Liquidsoap delta from a backend wall-clock absolute seek anchor instead of Liquidsoap raw_elapsed.
- Reset the seek anchor after every successful seek restart and direct seek, preventing stale segment-local cursor time from making later seeks land several seconds early or late.

Version 2311 - 2026-04-26
- Fixed repeated seek drift by switching second and later seeks to a backend-assisted Liquidsoap absolute seek path.
- Added a one-shot Liquidsoap source seek-base override so absolute UI targets are converted with the correct segment base even when metadata is temporarily stale or empty.
- Removed Python-side wall-clock delta from direct re-seek calculation to prevent request latency and buffer time from being added to the requested seek position.

Version 2312 - 2026-04-26
- Disabled the direct Liquidsoap live-source re-seek path because it can operate on a transient buffered/cued segment duration after a previous seek.
- Routed every manual seek through a fresh absolute cue-in playlist restart; active re-seeks remain isolated so stale playlist cursors cannot jump into the queue tail.
- Preserved the existing UI progress monotonic guard and EOF/crossfade deferred queue reload behavior.

Version 2313 - 2026-04-26
- Fixed repeated seek after a previous seek restart by isolating every manual seek playlist, not only active re-seeks.
- Added a short post-reload settle delay before skip so Liquidsoap cannot apply the skip to the previously cached seek+queue-tail cursor.
- Kept the real queue restore deferred until the seeked track EOF/crossfade window to preserve post-seek fade behavior.

Version 2351 - 2026-04-29
- Fixed post-seek fade preservation: seek restart playlist entries now carry the original fade-out metadata so the seeked track can still fade/crossfade into the next track.
- Stored live Liquidsoap fade metadata in the now-playing state and added a database fallback from cue_fade_start_seconds when rebuilding seek restart annotations.
- Added the explicit seek no-fade marker only for seek entry start-up so the seek jump stays hard-cut while the track end keeps its normal fade.


v2352 - 2026-04-29
- Fixed seek restart fade handling: seek no-fade metadata now disables only the entering fade-in and no longer clears Liquidsoap cross-start metadata, so end-of-track fade/crossfade remains active after seek.
- Kept seek restart cue/fade metadata intact for the restarted track.

Version 2353 - 2026-04-29
- Fixed seek restart fade regression: seek playlist entries no longer carry wb_seek_nofade, so Liquidsoap does not treat the seeked track as a no-overlap ending.
- Kept cue/fade-out metadata on seek restart entries so the seeked track can still fade normally at its end.

Version 2354 - 2026-04-29
- Fixed post-seek fade stability by restoring the real playlist queue earlier after an isolated seek restart, giving Liquidsoap enough time to preload the next source before the crossfade window.
- Kept the seek restart itself isolated so repeated seek operations cannot jump into the queue tail, while making the end-of-track fade/crossfade reliable again.

Version 2355 - 2026-04-29
- Moved post-seek end-fade ownership to Liquidsoap metadata: the crossfade transition now reads liq_fade_out/fade_out from the ending track and uses it for the handoff duration.
- Seek restarts now keep the normal queue tail visible in the M3U so Liquidsoap can preload the next request from the start of the restarted segment instead of relying on a Python timed playlist.reload.
- Kept Python responsible only for seek position/cue metadata calculation while Liquidsoap decides the actual fade timing at track end.

Version 2356 - 2026-04-29
- Added a near-end seek guard: manual seeks are ignored when the requested target would leave less than the safe end-handoff window in the current track.
- Logged dropped near-EOF seek requests with remaining time and guard values so future logs clearly show when the protection was applied.
- Kept the existing Liquidsoap metadata-based post-seek fade logic unchanged for normal seek positions.

Version 2357 - 2026-04-29
- Increased the near-end seek guard to a conservative handoff window so 16-second post-seek fragments are ignored instead of being restarted.
- Kept seek requests as successful no-ops when dropped by the guard, with debug logging for remaining time and guard threshold.
- Prevented very short seek restart segments from causing encoder mount loss and off-air automation triggers.

Version 2358 - 2026-04-29
- Fixed rapid repeated seek race conditions by ignoring new manual seek requests while a seek restart is active or in its short cooldown window.
- Cancelled stale seek restore, pin, grace and owner state before accepting a new seek so delayed restore callbacks cannot collide with the current restart.
- Added debug logging for seek requests dropped during active seek cooldown to make future diagnosis clear.

Version 2359 - 2026-04-29
- Fixed seek restart audio isolation so queued tail tracks cannot become audible during the restart window.
- Restored the real queue only near the seeked track EOF/crossfade window, instead of after a fixed short delay, to keep post-seek fade stable without brief wrong-track playback.
- Updated seek restore logging to identify the isolated seek then deferred reload path.

Version 2389 - 2026-05-01
- Re-enabled manual seek across the full visible track timeline by disabling the legacy near-EOF drop guard.
- Added dynamic early queue restore timing for near-end seek restarts so Liquidsoap can preload the next item before EOF without blocking last-seconds seeking.
- Kept exact-EOF clamping in place to avoid accidental next-track jumps while allowing seeks through the full track length.

Version 2390 - 2026-05-01

- Prevented near-end backward seeks from being misclassified as forward seeks, avoiding premature queue reloads that could jump to the second queued item.
- Kept full-length seeking and existing fade behavior unchanged.

Version 2391 - 2026-05-01

- Added a duplicate guarded seek restart entry so a near-EOF preloaded next request cannot jump to the second queue item during a backward seek.

Version 2392 - 2026-05-01

- Kept backward re-seek behavior immediate by letting a new seek overwrite the temporary helper playlist before queue restore.

Version 2393 - 2026-05-02

- Changed the hold entry to use the stored seek request cue-out as its deterministic anchor, never a runtime playhead snapshot.
- Shortened the safety hold slice to minimize any fallback repeat if Liquidsoap still reaches it.

Version 2394 - 2026-05-02

Version 2395 - 2026-05-02

- Passed the active seek generation into callback-scheduled queue restores so older callbacks cannot schedule uncancellable restore work after a newer accepted seek.
- Added final generation/path checks before deferred playlist reloads clear state, preventing stale restore threads from disturbing the current seek/fade flow.

Version 2396 - 2026-05-02
- Changed manual NEXT/SKIP to defer queue consumption until the authoritative Liquidsoap track-start callback reports the actual started entry.
- Removed optimistic NOW_PLAYING updates, immediate queue deletion, and playlist reload from the NEXT path to prevent skipped or duplicated tracks after manual NEXT and scripted inserts.

Version 2397 - 2026-05-02

Version 2398 - 2026-05-02

- Prevented the script engine from restarting an auto-start script that was manually stopped while the station remains ON AIR.

Version 2399 - 2026-05-02
- Prevented the scheduler from catching up stale missed occurrences when it starts or is re-enabled after the scheduled time has already passed.
- Added an overdue normalization guard that rolls recurring scheduler rules forward to their next future run instead of executing them immediately.
- Disabled stale one-shot scheduler rules instead of running them late; normal polling jitter within the grace window can still fire as expected.

v2400 - 2026-05-05 - Seek logic optimized for faster, accurate seek, direct seek conditional, adaptive cooldown, frontend sync improvements.

v2401 - 2026-05-05 - Seek after crossfade prewarm enabled, next track buffered for immediate mix after seek.

v2402 - 2026-05-18 - Fixed in-track seek target handling by making the numeric target_pos authoritative and using target_label only as a fallback. Shortened the accepted seek cooldown so repeated in-track seek clicks are not ignored for several seconds while singleflight protection still prevents overlapping restarts.

v2403 - 2026-05-18 - Added explicit post-seek next-stream prewarm scheduling: seek restarts now launch short repeated prewarm re-checks immediately after restart and again after queue restore, so near-end seeks can preload the next queued stream instead of waiting for the background poller.

v2404 - 2026-05-18 - Converted near-EOF manual seeks into a fade-to-next transition: seeks into the last fade window now restore the real queue and trigger Liquidsoap's playlist source through the existing crossfade chain instead of doing a restart plus hard skip.

v2405 - 2026-05-18 - Changed near-EOF seek fade-next handling to skip the audible radio graph instead of only the raw playlist source, so the next queued track starts immediately from its autocue cue-in point without a silence gap or fade-in.

v2406 - 2026-05-18 - Reworked near-EOF seek handoff to use a new start_next_now Liquidsoap command instead of fade_next. The command advances the live playlist source without pre-reloading the M3U, allowing the existing cross transition to overlap the current fade-out with the next track starting immediately at its autocue cue-in point. Deferred the queue file refresh until after the audible transition request to avoid fade-out-to-silence gaps caused by playlist reload timing.

v2407 - 2026-05-18 - Changed near-EOF manual seek behavior so it always seeks to the requested in-track position first. Removed the immediate start_next_now/fade_next conversion from the seek path; the next track is now allowed to start only from the normal cue-out/EOF handoff after the seeked segment plays out, with the existing queue restore/prewarm timing preparing the natural transition.

v2408 - 2026-05-18 - Changed near-EOF seek handling to use direct Liquidsoap seek_abs instead of the seek-restart playlist plus radio.skip path. This keeps the current playlist/crossfade source chain alive so the seeked position plays first and the next track can transition at natural cue-out/EOF. Added seek_direct_abs_near_eof_completed diagnostics to confirm the non-skip path.

v2409 - 2026-05-18 - Permanently disabled the direct Liquidsoap seek_abs path because it can restart the current track from the beginning or advance to the next track in this graph. Manual seek now always falls back to the restart-based seek implementation, including near-EOF seeks, with explicit diagnostics showing seek_abs is bypassed.

v2410 - 2026-05-18 - Restored restart-based seek with the real queue tail kept visible in the seek-restart playlist. This keeps the stable seek implementation while allowing Liquidsoap cross() to request the next real track during the seeked segment's cue-out/fade window instead of falling back to an isolated hold/silence path.

v2411 - 2026-05-18 - Reverted the v2410 seek-restart playlist tail injection. Seek restart playlists are isolated again and contain only the seeked current track, preventing radio.skip from jumping to the second queue item during seek binding. Kept seek_abs disabled and retained the restart-based seek path.

v2412 - 2026-05-18 - Added a seek handoff playlist restore path that keeps the seeked virtual segment as the first restored M3U entry and exposes the real queue tail only after the restart skip has already bound to the seeked segment. This keeps the M3U as the master queue while approximating an A/B virtual-player handoff: current seek segment remains player A, next queue item becomes player B for cue-out crossfade preparation, without putting the queue tail into the initial restart playlist.

v2416 - 2026-05-18 - Restored the v2412 full seek-handoff fade layout and added a now_playing duplicate guard for seek virtual segments. If Liquidsoap reports the same path/cue-in/cue-out handoff segment a second time after queue restore, the callback is ignored and the player is advanced immediately so the duplicate does not dequeue/history as a real track while the fade-preparation segment remains intact.

v2417 - 2026-05-18 - Reworked near-EOF restart seek handoff into an A/B-style virtual playlist shape: the seeked current segment is written twice, followed by the real queue tail. The first slot primes the post-reload cursor, the second slot is the audible current player, and the next queue item is available immediately for Liquidsoap crossfade without a deferred playlist reload. Disabled the deferred seek queue restore for isolated seek playlists to avoid the reload/reset race that removed fade in v2414-v2416.


v2418 - 2026-05-18
- Implemented true A/B virtual-player architecture using two Liquidsoap request.queue players (wb_player_a / wb_player_b).
- Local music playback now boots A as the current player and preloads B as the next player; cue-out transitions call ab.to_a / ab.to_b without playlist reload.
- Seek restart now replaces the active A/B player request directly instead of using radio.reload/radio.skip.
- Removed the previous duplicate-current playlist handoff from the active seek path.

2026-05-19 | v2463 | Added true Liquidsoap-side A/B overlap at cue-out: the next deck is prepared at its cue-in point, Python no longer overwrites Liquidsoap autocue cue-out/fade metadata with synthetic full-duration values, and the local A/B router crossfades before it is wrapped in mksafe or the main output switch.
2026-05-19 | v2464 | Fixed Liquidsoap-owned A/B handoff after near-end seek: A/B slots now become fallible when empty/consumed instead of returning long blank audio, and Python always sends a concrete cue-out/deadline and fade value to Liquidsoap so the prepared opposite deck starts instead of the same deck stalling or restarting.

- Added Python rebootstrap after queue mutations so the fresh queue head is loaded immediately into A/B slots instead of waiting for legacy playlist reload behavior.

v2550 - 2026-05-25
- Fixed UI queue mutation handling so queue add/remove/reorder no longer force-resets the currently audible A/B deck.
- Queue edits now preserve the on-air track as the current A/B plan item and replace only the inactive/preload deck with the first fresh queue item, so newly added queue items play after the current song instead of cutting it off.
- If the edited queue is empty, only the inactive deck is cleared while the active track continues to its normal cue-out/end.


v2551 - 2026-05-25
- Fixed rapid/near-end manual seek handling in the A/B Liquidsoap engine.
- Manual seek now force-completes any active overlap transition and releases the old deck.
- After seek, the inactive deck is refreshed from the next queue item, and near cue-out seeks can immediately start the prepared next deck to avoid silence.

Web Broadcaster v2552 - 2026-05-25
- Added a post-seek A/B graph settle window so overlap cannot start on the same scheduler tick as manual seek/release.
- Seek now force-completes transition, resyncs logical timing, then delays the next overlap until the graph and external audio pipe stabilize.
- Added Liquidsoap logging for delayed seek-settle transitions.

Web Broadcaster v2553 - 2026-05-25
- Fixed manual seek recovery when Liquidsoap invalidates the active request after rapid or near-end seeks.
- The active A/B deck can now re-arm its current URI once after seek and immediately seek the freshly decoded request back to the requested absolute position.
- Kept stale inactive-deck replay blocked, so old queue items cannot be reintroduced while preventing silent active-deck consumed-slot states.

Web Broadcaster v2554 - 2026-05-26
- Fixed rapid and near-end manual seek recovery in the A/B Liquidsoap engine by keeping a short seek rearm lease active after metadata has fired.
- The active deck may replay and re-seek its current URI during this lease if Liquidsoap consumes or invalidates the decoded request again, preventing silent consumed-slot states.
- Added automatic expiry logging for the seek rearm lease while preserving stale inactive-deck replay protection.

Web Broadcaster v2555 - 2026-05-26
- Fixed A/B active-deck recovery when Liquidsoap still reports stale metadata/request state after repeated seeks and handoff.
- Added a guarded one-shot active deck rescue replay so the audible decoder is forced back to the current UI/AB URI instead of remaining silent or leaking an older request.
- Kept stale inactive-deck replay blocked and reset the rescue lease on explicit load, clear, reset and seek operations.

v2556 - 2026-05-26
- Fixed stale decoded request leak at A/B transition start after repeated/near-end seeks.
- The target deck is now physically flushed and re-armed immediately before becoming audible, so only the current queue/UI URI can play.

v2557 - 2026-05-26
- Fixed manual seek accuracy in the A/B Liquidsoap engine by using a hard decoder rearm instead of calculating seek delta from the possibly stale logical clock.
- Active deck seeks now reload the current URI and re-seek the freshly decoded request to the requested absolute position, so repeated and 0:01 seeks cannot leave UI time and audio position out of sync.
- Removed the transition-edge target skip introduced in v2556; the preloaded target deck is kept intact and only a cue-in re-seek lease is armed, preventing the wrong old decoded request from replacing the next track.

v2558 - 2026-05-26
- Fixed stale A/B request duplication that could make the first track play again as the third track.
- Removed the generic active-deck rescue replay path from Liquidsoap request.dynamic; only explicit manual seek rearm may replay the current URI.
- This prevents consumed active slots from queuing a duplicate old URI ahead of the intended next deck while keeping seek recovery intact.

v2559 - 2026-05-26
- Fixed stale physical decoder state that could make the first track play again when the deck was reused as the third track.
- A/B preload now binds the freshly loaded URI into the physical decoder immediately after flushing the previous request.
- Transition start also re-arms the target decoder with the current URI so an old decoded request cannot remain at the head of the deck.

v2560 - 2026-05-26
- Fixed A/B deck stale decoder/request replay where track 1 could return as track 3.
- Removed preload/transition source.skip calls that consumed or duplicated the newly armed target URI.
- Added release-time decoder flush for old decks after overlap completion, forced activation, manual seek force-complete, and explicit clear.
- Ensured playback order follows the queue by only arming the current queue URI during preload and flushing stale physical decoders when a deck is released.

v2561 - 2026-05-26
- Fixed A/B deck empty-request starvation introduced in v2560 where consumed slots could push an invalid empty URI and leave the stream silent.
- Changed deck reload binding so the current queue URI is armed before the physical decoder skip, preventing old requests from returning while avoiding empty request loops.
- Added a tiny valid emergency empty-slot WAV only for unexpected empty pulls; normal queue playback always rebinds the real current URI before a deck becomes audible.

v2562 - 2026-05-26
- Fixed A/B stale decoder leakage where the first track could become audible again when the physical deck was reused as the third queue item.
- Added an authoritative expected-path guard for each Liquidsoap A/B deck; metadata from any decoded file that does not match the current queue path is skipped immediately and cannot open the audio gate.
- The final A/B audio gate now opens only after metadata for the expected queue path arrives, ensuring playback follows queue order even if Liquidsoap still has an old decoded request buffered.

v2563 - 2026-05-26

v2843 - 2026-07-12
- Added per-track SoundFile reference-level analysis using the 90th percentile of active 50 ms RMS windows.
- Cue-in and cue-out settings are now relative dB offsets from each track's own reference level; existing negative database values such as -34.0 and -38.0 remain compatible.
- Cue points, true audio boundaries and the reference level are calculated from one full-file decode, and analysis logs now include the reference level plus effective absolute thresholds.
- Bumped the analysis cache version so previously stored absolute-threshold cue results are automatically reanalyzed.
- Updated APP_VERSION to 2843.

v2844 - 2026-07-12
- Replaced cue-point matching with a fixed SAM-style SoundFile Gap Killer and one-sided crossfade system.
- Gap Killer now starts tracks at the first sustained signal above -20 dBFS, ignores blips of 10 ms or less, removes the trailing section below -24 dBFS, and ends a track at the start of an internal silence lasting at least 15 seconds.
- Crossfade timing is now determined only from the outgoing track: the trigger is detected 7 dB below the track reference level, with a 3.0-second fallback and a 0.1- to 6.0-second crossfade window.
- The incoming track starts immediately at its Gap Killer start point with no fade-in; the outgoing track uses a fixed 5.0-second, 90%-depth S-curve fade and is released at its effective end.
- Tracks with an effective duration of 65 seconds or less use a hard no-crossfade boundary while retaining Gap Killer trimming.
- Updated the Python safety timing and Liquidsoap A/B timing to use the same outgoing-trigger model, and bumped the analysis cache version so old cue results are reanalyzed automatically.
- Updated APP_VERSION to 2844.

v2845 - 2026-07-12
- Changed the outgoing SAM-style S-curve fade from 90% depth to complete zero gain so the decoder is never hard-cut while audible.
- Added a fixed 50 ms zero-gain safety hold before releasing the outgoing deck with source.skip, preventing end-of-track waveform discontinuity clicks.
- Kept Gap Killer thresholds, crossfade trigger analysis, incoming no-fade behavior, short-item handling and all other playback logic unchanged.
- Updated APP_VERSION to 2845.

v2846 - 2026-07-12

- This preserves quiet musical endings and removes the hard-cut impression while retaining the click protection, Gap Killer thresholds, trigger timing, incoming no-fade behavior and short-item rules.

v5020 - 2026-07-16
- Copied the active Liquidsoap A/B fade implementation into the native Icecast mixer without inventing a new crossfade model.
- The incoming deck now uses the same 20 ms smoothstep anti-click entry ramp as Liquidsoap and then remains at full gain.
- The outgoing deck now uses the same smoothstep fade-out curve and exact per-track wb_fade_duration timing as Liquidsoap.
- Added the same 50 ms silent release hold before the old deck is retired, including the 20 ms soft-no-crossfade handoff behavior.
- Liquidsoap now sends its authoritative transition start wall-clock, fade-out, entry-ramp, release-duration and silence-hold values with the A/B track-start callback.
- Native transition timing compensates for callback transport latency by mapping the original Liquidsoap transition start wall-clock onto the synchronized monotonic clock.
- Hard-clean handoffs now skip the fade and retire the old native voice immediately, matching the existing Liquidsoap branch.
- Added native Icecast transition diagnostics for the exact fade-out, entry-ramp, silence-hold, release duration and smoothstep curve.
- Updated APP_VERSION and native daemon version to 5020.

v5021 - 2026-07-16
- Split the native track timing descriptor into audio_start_ms, play_start_ms, transition_at_ms, effective_end_ms and source_end_ms while retaining cue_in_ms/cue_out_ms as compatibility aliases.
- Fixed native FFmpeg decoding so wb_crossfade_trigger starts the transition but no longer truncates PCM decoding at that point.
- Native decoding now continues from play_start_ms to wb_orig_total/source_end_ms, allowing the outgoing deck to keep supplying PCM throughout the Liquidsoap fade.
- Changed early-EOF validation to use the effective audible end rather than the crossfade trigger.
- Changed native seek clamping to use the physical source end, so seeking beyond the transition trigger remains valid.
- Added full timing-boundary diagnostics to native probe events and get_state, plus descriptor-mismatch details for all new boundaries.
- Added regression coverage proving that a 500 ms transition trigger on a 2 second source still decodes the complete tail and that seek can move beyond the transition point.
- Updated APP_VERSION and native daemon version to 5021.

v5022 - 2026-07-16
- Added separate normal-start and seek-restart deadlines: normal voices retain the 500 ms mismatch threshold, while seek restarts remain pending for up to 1200 ms by default.
- Added native_audio_probe_seek_pending as a non-terminal diagnostic so a slow but valid seek restart no longer increments audio_shadow_mismatch_count.

- Fixed same-deck hard handoffs so PCM still arriving from the retired logical voice is counted as an intentional stale drop instead of a FIFO overrun.
- Kept silence_frames as the total encoded silent-frame counter while distinguishing idle silence from a missing audible deck.

v5023 - 2026-07-16

- Added explicit rejection and counters for late PCM from the retired seek generation.

v5024 - 2026-07-16
- Kept the synchronized Liquidsoap fade-out start, duration and smoothstep curve unchanged.
- Fixed the incoming native deck anti-click ramp so it starts on the first actual PCM frame instead of expiring while the decoder callback is still in flight.
- Added a PCM-gated transition entry state: the incoming deck remains at zero gain until its exact slot-token PCM reaches the Icecast FIFO.

- Added adaptive near-boundary monitor timing with a 4 ms command lead so the prepared target is selected at audio_end without running the high-frequency loop for the whole track.

v5074 - 2026-07-19
------------------

- Primed the incoming deck with 80 ms of exact PCM and switched between outgoing and incoming samples atomically inside the 20 ms mixer tick, with zero fade and zero overlap.
- Corrected normal hard-handoff timing for PCM already queued in the outgoing mixer FIFO, preventing the previous song from being cut at its decoder-ahead position.
- Made active terminal EOF claim an armed handoff, drain every remaining outgoing FIFO frame and continue with the primed target without an output-silence tick.
- Kept the old token-scoped Python hard select only as a fallback when native handoff arming is unavailable or fails.

- Added in-process MP3 encoding through libmp3lame and AAC-LC/HE-AAC/HE-AACv2 encoding through libfdk_aac while retaining the existing per-output encoded FIFOs, Icecast connections, reconnect behavior and metadata ownership.
- Added a self-contained FFmpeg SDK header set and pinned native-engine RPATH so the daemon always loads the bundled custom libraries from bin/ffmpeg without LD_LIBRARY_PATH or a system FFmpeg fallback.
- Removed all FFmpeg fork/exec paths from live decoding, analysis and encoding; one station now creates no FFmpeg child process, while the optional external DSP remains visible only when enabled.
- Added embedded-libav runtime and process-model diagnostics while keeping the existing ffmpeg_* protocol fields for backward-compatible UI and telemetry consumers.
- Updated APP_VERSION and native daemon version to 6000.

v6001 - 2026-07-20
------------------
- Added the canonical Ice-Audio-Info source header to every native Icecast connection so Icecast exposes audio_info and compatible players receive reliable stream-format information.
- Built the header independently per output from the configured bitrate while preserving the native 44.1 kHz stereo PCM contract: ice-samplerate=44100;ice-bitrate=<output bitrate>;ice-channels=2.
- Added regression coverage for MP3, AAC+ and simultaneous multi-output handshakes, including distinct bitrate values on different mounts.
- Preserved the v6000 embedded-libav audio path, external SoundSolution DSP process, reconnect behavior, metadata updates and zero-FFmpeg-process model unchanged.
- Updated APP_VERSION and native daemon version to 6001.

v6002 - 2026-07-21
------------------
- Removed the legacy DEBUG=1 and WEB_BROADCASTER_DEBUG runtime modes and their alternate Python logging configuration.
- Removed debug-only web_broadcaster.log and startstop log handling, browser client-trace beacons and endpoint, Settings Console debug gating, and script, scheduler, history, queue and search debug call paths.
- Removed debug callback fields from the AutoDJ, playback repository, lifecycle, player orchestration and station service module boundaries.
- Kept warning, error and exception logging, logs/native_engine.log, and the structured logs/audio_engine_protocol.jsonl protocol telemetry.
- Made bounded reusable SQLite connections unconditional so diagnostics no longer change the production database connection model.
- Replaced old debug-string regressions with behavioral guards for stopped stations, pending seek, native hard handoff and delayed preload safety.
- Preserved the v6001 Ice-Audio-Info headers, v6000 embedded-libav audio path, external SoundSolution DSP, playback, seek, Manual Next, AutoDJ and Icecast behavior unchanged.
- Updated APP_VERSION and native daemon version to 6002.

v6003 - 2026-07-21
------------------
- Made embedded libav decoding tolerant of isolated AVERROR_INVALIDDATA failures for local files as well as network streams, matching the former FFmpeg CLI behavior of skipping damaged MP3 packets or frames and resynchronizing on later valid audio.
- Added a bounded 256-error consecutive corrupt-input guard so recoverable damage cannot turn into an infinite decoder loop; successful decoded frames reset the guard.
- Added corrupt_input_skipped_count terminal telemetry to native probe EOF, early-EOF and decoder-error events.
- Changed unrecoverable active-track decoder failures to drain already decoded PCM, mark the outgoing track terminal, wake native timing and request the next deck immediately instead of leaving the station on permanent silence.
- Allowed terminal recovery even when full-file PCM analysis failed on the same damaged media, while preserving normal analysis requirements for non-terminal playback.
- Added a real damaged-MP3 regression that verifies native PCM analysis and playback both recover, reach EOF and report skipped corrupt input without a decoder error.
- Preserved the v6002 debug cleanup, v6001 Ice-Audio-Info headers, v6000 embedded-libav path, external SoundSolution DSP, seek, Manual Next, AutoDJ and Icecast behavior unchanged.
- Updated APP_VERSION and native daemon version to 6003.

v6004 - 2026-07-21
------------------
- Added separate Ice-Bitrate, Ice-Description, Ice-Genre and Ice-URL source headers to every native Icecast connection while preserving the existing Ice-Audio-Info header.
- Passed the persisted icecast_streams bitrate, station_description, genre and website_url values through the Python native-engine API and daemon protocol into each individual output configuration.
- Added CR/LF sanitization for all user-configurable Icecast source header values to prevent header injection without changing the stored values.
- Exposed stream_description, stream_genre and stream_url in native Icecast output state for diagnostics and multi-output verification.
- Added regressions for database-to-native field routing and the complete MP3 source handshake metadata.
- Preserved the v6003 corrupt-audio recovery, v6002 debug cleanup, embedded-libav path, external SoundSolution DSP, seek, Manual Next, AutoDJ and Icecast reconnect behavior unchanged.
- Updated APP_VERSION and native daemon version to 6004.

v6005 - 2026-07-21
------------------
- Removed the optional User-Agent header from the native Icecast SOURCE handshake so Icecast no longer publishes the redundant source-client user_agent statistic.
- Removed the same unnecessary User-Agent header from native Icecast metadata admin requests for one consistent minimal HTTP request policy.
- Added regressions proving source streaming, metadata updates and complete Icecast identity headers work without any User-Agent field.
- Preserved the v6004 bitrate, description, genre, URL and audio_info headers, v6003 corrupt-audio recovery, embedded-libav path, external SoundSolution DSP, reconnect, seek, Manual Next and AutoDJ behavior unchanged.
- Updated APP_VERSION and native daemon version to 6005.

v6007 - 2026-07-21

- Made newly created Autostart encoders start immediately when their station is already ON AIR, and applied the same behavior when Autostart is enabled while configuring a stopped encoder.
- Allowed the station-wide SoundSolution DSP checkbox to be changed while ON AIR while keeping every other General and Crossfade setting OFF-AIR-only.
- Applied live DSP changes by reconfiguring the shared native encoder/DSP pipeline without stopping deck playback or restarting the station; existing enabled Icecast outputs remain owned by their stable stream IDs.
- Added regressions for immediate Autostart, permanent popup removal, ON-AIR settings restrictions and live DSP reconfiguration.

v6008 - 2026-07-22
------------------
- Reworked the embedded libav multi-output encoder group so individual stream branches can be attached and detached while the shared PCM reader remains running.
- Made ON-AIR encoder creation and re-enabling add only the requested MP3 or AAC+ branch without restarting existing Icecast connections, the shared encoder pipeline or the external SoundSolution DSP process.
- Made encoder deletion and disable detach only the selected branch while every other stream keeps its existing connection, encoder generation and uninterrupted encoded-data flow.
- Added a dedicated encoder-control mutex and fixed slot-indexed branch storage to protect live branch lifetime without adding per-output mixer, DSP or PCM-reader threads.
- Kept full shared-pipeline reconfiguration only for changes that genuinely affect the common DSP path or an already-running encoder's codec configuration.
- Added real MP3/AAC+ regressions for ON-AIR add, delete and re-add, including a shared-DSP test proving stable DSP PID, stable encoder generation, zero pipeline restarts and no reconnect of the unaffected stream.
- Preserved the v6007 immediate Autostart and live DSP controls, v6006 crossfade cleanup, embedded-libav audio path, metadata, seek, Manual Next and AutoDJ behavior unchanged.
- Updated APP_VERSION and native daemon version to 6008.

v6009 - 2026-07-22
------------------
- Decoupled the external SoundSolution stdout pipe from the embedded libav encoder group so every running encoder keeps one stable push-based PCM input and continuous PTS timeline.
- Added a live dry-to-DSP and DSP-to-dry PCM source selector protected by a dedicated route mutex; DSP enable keeps dry audio flowing while SoundSolution starts and switches only after processed PCM is ready.
- Made ON-AIR DSP enable and disable preserve encoder generation, pipeline restart count, encoded FIFO ownership and every existing Icecast SOURCE connection.
- Added a dedicated SoundSolution output-reader thread, process-generation guards, failure recovery and route telemetry without changing the 44.1 kHz, 16-bit stereo external DSP contract.
- Added a real end-to-end regression that toggles DSP in both directions while proving uninterrupted encoded data, stable encoder generation, zero shared-pipeline restarts and a single Icecast connection.
- Preserved the v6008 dynamic encoder-branch lifecycle, v6007 immediate Autostart, v6006 crossfade cleanup, embedded-libav audio path, metadata, seek, Manual Next and AutoDJ behavior unchanged.
- Updated APP_VERSION and native daemon version to 6009.

- Added a dedicated DSP-only API so all other General and Crossfade fields remain isolated in the existing explicit Save workflow.
- Added browser-side in-flight locking, success feedback and automatic checkbox rollback when persistence or live DSP application fails.
- Reset the normal DSP input-stall watchdog when the first processed PCM arrives, so legitimate slow SoundSolution startup cannot inherit startup backpressure age and trigger a false pipeline restart.
- Preserved the v6009 live dry/DSP PCM source selector, continuous encoder PTS, stable Icecast connections and external SoundSolution process contract unchanged.

v6012 - 2026-07-22

- Replaced the Wine/AppImage SoundSolution process with the bundled native Linux SoundSolution v0.5.0 runtime.

- Reinterpreted the existing `ssproc_appimage` settings column as the station SoundSolution `.dat` path without changing the database schema; empty and v6011 AppImage values resolve to the bundled bit-exact `ss18.dat` configuration.
- Removed Wine, AppImage, FUSE, WINEPREFIX, HOME, cache and temporary runtime preparation from the DSP child while retaining station-scoped process ownership, stderr logging, startup gate, watchdog, restart, clean process-group shutdown and live dry/DSP source switching.

v6013 - 2026-07-22
-------------------
- Integrated SoundSolution Native v0.5.0 directly into the multi-station daemon through `libsoundsolution.so.0`; DSP audio is now processed in place before the embedded libav encoder.
- Each station owns an independent `ssnative_dsp` context loaded from its existing `.dat` configuration, preserving station isolation, live dry/DSP switching, failure recovery and metadata delay.
- Removed the active DSP subprocess, stdin/stdout pipe, fork/exec and DSP helper-thread path from runtime operation.
- Reduced the bundled DSP runtime to `bin/soundsolution/libsoundsolution.so.0.5.0`, its SONAME symlink and `ss18.dat`; no DSP executable, SDK, documentation or CPU-duplicate library copies are included.
- Added a private RPATH for `bin/soundsolution`.
- Updated APP_VERSION and native daemon version to 6013.


v6014 - 2026-07-23
-------------------
- Added a dedicated Werkzeug request handler that treats vanished HTTP clients as normal disconnects while writing or flushing responses.
- Suppressed only expected client-side socket failures such as EHOSTUNREACH, ENETUNREACH, EPIPE, ECONNRESET, ECONNABORTED and ETIMEDOUT; unrelated HTTP server exceptions remain visible.
- Prevented the harmless `OSError: [Errno 113] No route to host` traceback from appearing in the console without changing request handling, application availability, audio, DSP, encoder or Icecast behavior.
- Updated APP_VERSION and native daemon version to 6014.

- Prevented scheduled station scripts from interrupting an actively playing HTTP/HTTPS URL source.

- Made a blocked occurrence count as completed for its exact scheduled timestamp, so an hourly announcement skipped during URL playback is not replayed later.
- Added targeted rollback of only the queue rows created by the blocked script occurrence if URL playback starts during the scheduling race window.
- Preserved manual operator Next/Stop controls, normal file playback scripts, AutoDJ, DSP, encoders, Icecast and the existing DEBUG logging policy.

v6018 - 2026-07-25

v6019 - 2026-07-25
-------------------
- Kept Mutagen as the fast initial duration source and added one-time native duration verification after the first clean full-file EOF.
- Corrected materially wrong stored durations when the native absolute source position differs by at least one second, including clean early-EOF cases caused by an overestimated Mutagen length.
- Stored file size and modification time with the verified duration so an unchanged track is checked only once and a replaced file can be verified again.
- Preserved manual cue, fade and audio-boundary values by updating only endpoint fields that still match the old automatic full-file duration.
- Added non-blocking EOF processing, queue/UI refresh and safe inactive-deck replanning after an automatic correction.
- Updated APP_VERSION and native daemon version to 6019.


v6020 - 2026-07-25
-------------------
- Prevented a naturally finished or failed native decoder candidate from being reused as a shared preload for a later queue identity of the same file.
- Cleared prebuffer-ready state on every terminal probe transition and prohibited zero-byte EOF buffers from emitting prebuffer-ready events.
- Required shared candidates to remain live or own at least one complete PCM frame, with a second activation-time guard that opens a fresh decoder for terminal or empty identities.
- Added regression coverage for playing the same file again after natural EOF, verifying a non-empty fresh preload and a real second audio start.
- Updated APP_VERSION and native daemon version to 6020.


v6021 - 2026-07-26
-------------------
- Replaced every deprecated `datetime.utcnow()` call with timezone-aware UTC acquisition while preserving the existing naive UTC database and scheduler timestamp format.
- Added a narrow embedded-libav log callback that suppresses only the repeated MP3 decoder `Header missing` console line from `mp3float`; all other libav errors remain visible.
- Updated APP_VERSION and native daemon version to 6021.

v6022 - 2026-07-28
-------------------
- Replaced the bundled SoundSolution Native v0.5.0 runtime with the final SoundSolution Native v6.1.0 x86-64 SSE2-direct production engine.
- Embedded the final SoundSolution Native v6.1.0 SSE2 runtime as one flat, regular `bin/soundsolution/libsoundsolution.so.2` ELF file, together with the synchronized v6.1.0 C API header and `ss18.dat`; no SONAME symlink, duplicate versioned library, external DSP archive or build-time library replacement is required.
- Relinked the native multi-station daemon directly against `libsoundsolution.so.2` while preserving the in-process S16LE stereo 44.1 kHz DSP API, station isolation, live dry/DSP switching and private-library RPATH.
- Added runtime regression coverage for SoundSolution version `6.1.0`, arithmetic mode `native-binary64-fast`, the new SONAME and real PCM processing.
- Made the Node.js-backed queue drag-and-drop execution check optional: it runs when `node` is installed and is reported as skipped otherwise; Node.js is not a Web Broadcaster build dependency.
- Updated APP_VERSION and native daemon version to 6022.

v6024 - 2026-07-28

- Added one self-contained Web Broadcaster buildkit for Debian 12 amd64 and native Raspberry Pi 5 ARM64 builds.

- Preserved the v6023 final package contract exactly: the application, PyInstaller runtime, native daemon, private FFmpeg libraries, `libsoundsolution.so.2` and `ss18.dat` remain in the flat `bin/` layout.
- Kept the validated Raspberry Pi 5 SoundSolution v6.0.0 structured-process runtime byte-for-byte unchanged: it is never rebuilt, stripped, patched or otherwise modified by the Web Broadcaster build.
- Kept SoundSolution Native v6.1.0 SSE2-direct for amd64 and FFmpeg 7.1.5 with the matching generic-amd64 or Cortex-A76 runtime.
- Corrected the v6024 source regression paths for the external FFmpeg staging layout: the diagnostic executable is `bin/ffmpeg`, while the private shared libraries are under `lib/`.
- Made the one-deck native audio probe regression deterministic on loaded Debian 12 builders by ignoring preload-generation EOF events until the exact active `native_audio_probe_started` event has been observed.
